Documentation:

Method code:
157 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
158 |                                    String sourceString,
159 |                                    Config config,
160 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
161 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
162 |         config, errorReporter);
163 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
164 | 
165 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
166 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
167 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
168 |             !comment.isParsed()) {
169 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment);
170 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK) {
171 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
172 |         }
173 |       }
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
177 | 
178 |     return irNode;
179 |   }

===== M0004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:

126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:

243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 730-756
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
730 |   @Override
731 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
732 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
733 |       return false;
734 |     }
735 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
736 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
737 |       return false;
738 |     }
739 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
740 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
741 |         return this == that;
742 |       }
743 |       return false;
744 |     }
745 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
747 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
748 |       }
749 |       return false;
750 |     }
751 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
755 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
756 |   }

===== M0027 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.setSourceInfo(Node,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 296-313
Documentation:

Method code:
296 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
297 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
298 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
299 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
300 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
301 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
302 |     } else {
303 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
304 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
305 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
306 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
307 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
308 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
309 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
310 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
311 |       }
312 |     }
313 |   }

===== M0028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 456-483
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

===== M0029 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1005-1026
Documentation:

Method code:
1005 |   static boolean nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node n, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
1006 |     if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
1007 |       return true;
1008 |     }
1009 | 
1010 |     switch(n.getType()) {
1011 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1012 |       case Token.DEC:
1013 |       case Token.INC:
1014 |       case Token.THROW:
1015 |         return true;
1016 |       case Token.CALL:
1017 |         return NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler);
1018 |       case Token.NEW:
1019 |         return NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler);
1020 |       case Token.NAME:
1021 |         // A variable definition.
1022 |         return n.hasChildren();
1023 |       default:
1024 |         return false;
1025 |     }
1026 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1005-1118
Documentation:

Method code:
1005 |   private void toString(
1006 |       StringBuilder sb,
1007 |       boolean printSource,
1008 |       boolean printAnnotations,
1009 |       boolean printType) {
1010 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
1011 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
1012 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
1013 |         sb.append(' ');
1014 |         sb.append(getString());
1015 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
1016 |         sb.append(' ');
1017 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
1018 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
1019 |         // toStringTree.
1020 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
1021 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
1022 |         } else {
1023 |           sb.append(first.getString());
1024 |         }
1025 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
1026 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
1027 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
1028 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
1029 |           sb.append(' ');
1030 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
1031 |         }
1032 |         if (printSource) {
1033 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
1034 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
1035 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
1036 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
1037 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
1038 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
1039 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
1040 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
1041 |           sb.append(']');
1042 |         }
1043 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
1044 |         sb.append(' ');
1045 |         sb.append(getDouble());
1046 |       }
1047 |       if (printSource) {
1048 |         int lineno = getLineno();
1049 |         if (lineno != -1) {
1050 |           sb.append(' ');
1051 |           sb.append(lineno);
1052 |         }
1053 |       }
1054 | 
1055 |       if (printAnnotations) {
1056 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
1057 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
1058 |           int type = keys[i];
1059 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
1060 |           sb.append(" [");
1061 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
1062 |           sb.append(": ");
1063 |           String value;
1064 |           switch (type) {
1065 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
1066 |               value = "target block property";
1067 |               break;
1068 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
1069 |               value = "last local block";
1070 |               break;
1071 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
1072 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1073 |                 case BOTH:
1074 |                   value = "both";
1075 |                   break;
1076 |                 case RIGHT:
1077 |                   value = "right";
1078 |                   break;
1079 |                 case LEFT:
1080 |                   value = "left";
1081 |                   break;
1082 |                 default:
1083 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1084 |               }
1085 |               break;
1086 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1087 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1088 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1089 |                   value = "eval";
1090 |                   break;
1091 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1092 |                   value = "with";
1093 |                   break;
1094 |                 default:
1095 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1096 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1097 |               }
1098 |               break;
1099 |             default:
1100 |               value = x.toString();
1101 |               break;
1102 |           }
1103 |           sb.append(value);
1104 |           sb.append(']');
1105 |         }
1106 |       }
1107 | 
1108 |       if (printType) {
1109 |         if (jsType != null) {
1110 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1111 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1112 |             sb.append(" : ");
1113 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1114 |           }
1115 |         }
1116 |       }
1117 |     }
1118 |   }

   * Casts this to an ObjectType, or returns null if this is not an ObjectType.

===== M0039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 86-735
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(
100 |           childCount == 2,
101 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
102 |           opstr, childCount);
103 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
104 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
105 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
106 | 
107 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
108 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
109 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
110 | 
111 |       // Handle associativity.
112 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
113 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
114 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
115 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
118 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
119 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
120 |       } else {
121 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
122 |       }
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
127 | 
128 |     switch (type) {
129 |       case Token.TRY: {
130 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
131 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
132 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
133 | 
134 |         add("try");
135 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 | 
137 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
138 |         // isn't a catch block
139 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
140 |         if (catchblock != null) {
141 |           add(catchblock);
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         if (childCount == 3) {
145 |           add("finally");
146 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
147 |         }
148 |         break;
149 |       }
150 | 
151 |       case Token.CATCH:
152 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
153 |         add("catch(");
154 |         add(first);
155 |         add(")");
156 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
157 |         break;
158 | 
159 |       case Token.THROW:
160 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
161 |         add("throw");
162 |         add(first);
163 | 
164 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
165 |         // parse this.
166 |         cc.endStatement(true);
167 |         break;
168 | 
169 |       case Token.RETURN:
170 |         add("return");
171 |         if (childCount == 1) {
172 |           add(first);
173 |         } else {
174 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
175 |         }
176 |         cc.endStatement();
177 |         break;
178 | 
179 |       case Token.VAR:
180 |         if (first != null) {
181 |           add("var ");
182 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 | 
186 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
187 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
188 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
189 |         break;
190 | 
191 |       case Token.NAME:
192 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |         } else {
195 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
196 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
197 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
198 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
199 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
200 |           } else {
201 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
202 |             // precedence.
203 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
204 |           }
205 |         }
206 |         break;
207 | 
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |         add("[");
210 |         addArrayList(first);
211 |         add("]");
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.LP:
215 |         add("(");
216 |         addList(first);
217 |         add(")");
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.COMMA:
221 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
222 |         addList(first, false, context);
223 |         break;
224 | 
225 |       case Token.NUMBER:
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
227 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
231 |       case Token.VOID:
232 |       case Token.NOT:
233 |       case Token.BITNOT:
234 |       case Token.POS: {
235 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
237 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
238 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
239 |         break;
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       case Token.NEG: {
243 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
244 | 
245 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
246 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
247 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
248 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
249 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
250 |         } else {
251 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
252 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |         break;
256 |       }
257 | 
258 |       case Token.HOOK: {
259 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
260 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
261 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
262 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
263 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
264 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
265 |         addExpr(last, 1);
266 |         break;
267 |       }
268 | 
269 |       case Token.REGEXP:
270 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
271 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
272 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
276 | 
277 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
278 |         if (childCount == 2) {
279 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
280 |         } else {
281 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
282 |           add(regexp);
283 |         }
284 |         break;
285 | 
286 |       case Token.GET_REF:
287 |         add(first);
288 |         break;
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
292 |         add(first);
293 |         add(".");
294 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
295 |         break;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
298 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
299 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
300 |         }
301 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
302 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
303 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
304 |           add("(");
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         add("function");
308 |         add(first);
309 | 
310 |         add(first.getNext());
311 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
312 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
313 | 
314 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
315 |           add(")");
316 |         }
317 |         break;
318 | 
319 |       case Token.GET:
320 |       case Token.SET:
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
323 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
324 | 
325 |         // Get methods are unnamed
326 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
327 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
328 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
329 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
330 |           add("get ");
331 |         } else {
332 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
333 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
334 |           add("set ");
335 |         }
336 | 
337 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
338 |         String name = n.getString();
339 |         Node fn = first;
340 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
341 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
342 | 
343 |         // Add the property name.
344 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
345 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
346 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
347 |             // unicode escaped.
348 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
349 |           add(name);
350 |         } else {
351 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
352 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
353 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
354 |             cc.addNumber(d);
355 |           } else {
356 |             addJsString(n.getString());
357 |           }
358 |         }
359 | 
360 |         add(parameters);
361 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
362 |         break;
363 | 
364 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
365 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
366 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
367 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
368 |         }
369 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
370 |         if (preserveBlock) {
371 |           cc.beginBlock();
372 |         }
373 | 
374 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
375 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
376 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
377 |                 !preserveBlock &&
378 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
379 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
380 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
381 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
382 | 
383 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
384 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
385 |             cc.endStatement();
386 |           }
387 | 
388 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
389 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
390 |           }
391 | 
392 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
393 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
394 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
395 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
396 |           }
397 |         }
398 |         if (preserveBlock) {
399 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
400 |         }
401 |         break;
402 |       }
403 | 
404 |       case Token.FOR:
405 |         if (childCount == 4) {
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
408 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
409 |           } else {
410 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
411 |           }
412 |           add(";");
413 |           add(first.getNext());
414 |           add(";");
415 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
416 |           add(")");
417 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
418 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
419 |         } else {
420 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
421 |           add("for(");
422 |           add(first);
423 |           add("in");
424 |           add(first.getNext());
425 |           add(")");
426 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
427 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.DO:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("do");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(last);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         cc.endStatement();
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.WHILE:
442 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(first);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
447 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.EMPTY:
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
455 |         Preconditions.checkState(
456 |             childCount == 2,
457 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
458 |         Preconditions.checkState(
459 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
460 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
461 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
462 |         if (needsParens) {
463 |           add("(");
464 |         }
465 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
466 |         if (needsParens) {
467 |           add(")");
468 |         }
469 |         add(".");
470 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
471 |         break;
472 |       }
473 | 
474 |       case Token.GETELEM:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(
476 |             childCount == 2,
477 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
478 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
479 |         add("[");
480 |         add(first.getNext());
481 |         add("]");
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.WITH:
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
486 |         add("with(");
487 |         add(first);
488 |         add(")");
489 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
490 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.INC:
494 |       case Token.DEC: {
495 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
496 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
497 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
498 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
499 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
500 |         if (postProp != 0) {
501 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
502 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
503 |         } else {
504 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
505 |           add(first);
506 |         }
507 |         break;
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       case Token.CALL:
511 |         // We have two special cases here:
512 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
513 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
515 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
516 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
517 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
518 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
519 |         // that must be preserved.
520 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
521 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
522 |           add("(0,");
523 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
524 |           add(")");
525 |         } else {
526 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
527 |         }
528 |         add("(");
529 |         addList(first.getNext());
530 |         add(")");
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.IF:
534 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
535 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
536 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
537 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
538 |           cc.beginBlock();
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         add("if(");
542 |         add(first);
543 |         add(")");
544 | 
545 |         if (hasElse) {
546 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
547 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
548 |           add("else");
549 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
550 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
551 |         } else {
552 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
553 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
554 |         }
555 | 
556 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
557 |           cc.endBlock();
558 |         }
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.NULL:
562 |       case Token.THIS:
563 |       case Token.FALSE:
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
602 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
603 | 
604 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
605 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
606 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
607 |         cc.endStatement();
608 |         break;
609 | 
610 |       case Token.NEW:
611 |         add("new ");
612 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
613 | 
614 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
615 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
616 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
617 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }

85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 |

97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 |

104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0056 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

===== M0059 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0063 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2443-2453
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2443 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2444 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2445 |     int len = s.length();
2446 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2447 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2448 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2449 |         return false;

===== M0066 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 926-976
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
926 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
927 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
928 |     if (callNode.getType() != Token.CALL) {
929 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
930 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
931 |     }
932 | 
933 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
934 |       return false;
935 |     }
936 | 
937 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
938 | 
939 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
940 |     if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
941 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
942 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
943 |         return false;
944 |       }
945 |     } else if (nameNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
946 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
947 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
948 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
949 |         return false;
950 |       }
951 | 
952 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
953 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
954 |         return false;
955 |       }
956 | 
957 |       // Functions in the "Math" namespace have no side effects.
958 | 
959 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
960 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.REGEXP
961 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
962 |           return false;
963 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.STRING
964 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
965 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
966 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
967 |           if (param != null &&
968 |               (param.getType() == Token.STRING
969 |                   || param.getType() == Token.REGEXP))
970 |           return false;
971 |         }
972 |       }
973 |     }
974 | 
975 |     return true;
976 |   }

===== M0075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 431-483
Documentation:

Method code:
431 |   @Override
432 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
433 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
434 |       return true;
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     // Union types
438 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
439 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
440 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
441 |       return false;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     // record types
445 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
446 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Interfaces
450 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
451 |     // to the interface instance.
452 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
453 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
454 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
455 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
456 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
457 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
458 |           return true;
459 |         }
460 |       }
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
464 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
465 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
466 |           return true;
467 |         }
468 |       }
469 |     }
470 | 
471 |     // other prototype based objects
472 |     if (that != null) {
473 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
474 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
475 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
476 |         // to avoid guessing.
477 |         return true;
478 |       }
479 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     return false;
483 |   }

===== M0100 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 441-456
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

568 |     @Override
569 |     Node processFunctionCall(FunctionCall callNode) {
570 |       Node node = newNode(transformTokenType(callNode.getType()),
571 |                            transform(callNode.getTarget()));
572 |       for (AstNode child : callNode.getArguments()) {

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 153-273
Documentation:

Method code:
153 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
154 |     switch (n.getType()) {
155 |       // Childless expressions
156 |       case Token.FALSE:
157 |       case Token.NULL:
158 |       case Token.THIS:
159 |       case Token.TRUE:
160 |         validateChildless(n);
161 |         return;
162 | 
163 |       // General uniary ops
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.POS:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.INC:
169 |       case Token.DEC:
170 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
171 |       case Token.VOID:
172 |       case Token.BITNOT:
173 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
174 |         return;
175 | 
176 |       // General binary ops
177 |       case Token.COMMA:
178 |       case Token.OR:
179 |       case Token.AND:
180 |       case Token.BITOR:
181 |       case Token.BITXOR:
182 |       case Token.BITAND:
183 |       case Token.EQ:
184 |       case Token.NE:
185 |       case Token.SHEQ:
186 |       case Token.SHNE:
187 |       case Token.LT:
188 |       case Token.GT:
189 |       case Token.LE:
190 |       case Token.GE:
191 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
192 |       case Token.IN:
193 |       case Token.LSH:
194 |       case Token.RSH:
195 |       case Token.URSH:
196 |       case Token.SUB:
197 |       case Token.ADD:
198 |       case Token.MUL:
199 |       case Token.MOD:
200 |       case Token.DIV:
201 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
202 |         return;
203 | 
204 |       // Assignments
205 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
217 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
218 |         return;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.HOOK:
221 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
222 |         return;
223 | 
224 |       // Node types that require special handling
225 |       case Token.STRING:
226 |         validateString(n);
227 |         return;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.NUMBER:
230 |         validateNumber(n);
231 |         return;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.NAME:
234 |         validateName(n);
235 |         return;
236 | 
237 |       case Token.GETELEM:
238 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
239 |         return;
240 | 
241 |       case Token.GETPROP:
242 |         validateGetProp(n);
243 |         return;
244 | 
245 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
246 |         validateArrayLit(n);
247 |         return;
248 | 
249 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
250 |         validateObjectLit(n);
251 |         return;
252 | 
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
255 |         return;
256 | 
257 |       case Token.CALL:
258 |         validateCall(n);
259 |         return;
260 | 
261 |       case Token.NEW:
262 |         validateNew(n);
263 |         return;
264 | 
265 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
266 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
267 |         return;
268 | 
269 |       default:
270 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
271 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()), n);
272 |     }
273 |   }

===== M0120 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 420-436
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
420 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
421 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
422 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
423 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
424 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
425 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
426 |         String inputName = input.getName();
427 | 
428 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
429 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
430 |         // will get an error down the line.
431 |         inputs.add(input);
432 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
433 |       }
434 |     }
435 |     return inputs;
436 |   }

===== M0130 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
920 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
921 |     int count = 0;
922 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
923 |       count++;
924 |     }
925 |

928 |       count--;

930 |     }
931 | 
932 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
933 |     return keys;
934 |   }

===== M0134 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.tryOptimizeBlock(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 446-473
Documentation:
/**
   * Try removing unneeded block nodes and their useless children
   */
Method code:
446 |   Node tryOptimizeBlock(Node n) {
447 |     // Remove any useless children
448 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; ) {
449 |       Node next = c.getNext();  // save c.next, since 'c' may be removed
450 |       if (!mayHaveSideEffects(c) && !c.isSyntheticBlock()) {
451 |         // TODO(johnlenz): determine what this is actually removing. Candidates
452 |         //    include: EMPTY nodes, control structures without children
453 |         //    (removing infinite loops), empty try blocks.  What else?
454 |         n.removeChild(c);  // lazy kids
455 |         reportCodeChange();
456 |       } else {
457 |         tryOptimizeConditionalAfterAssign(c);
458 |       }
459 |       c = next;
460 |     }
461 | 
462 |     if (n.isSyntheticBlock() ||  n.getParent() == null) {
463 |       return n;
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     // Try to remove the block.
467 |     if (NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(n)) {
468 |       reportCodeChange();
469 |       return null;
470 |     }
471 | 
472 |     return n;
473 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *

===== M0153 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1234-1242
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1234 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1235 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1236 |       return -1;
1237 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1238 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1239 |     } else {
1240 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1241 |     }
1242 |   }

   */

1091 |   Node parseInputs() {

1113 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1114 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1115 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1116 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1117 |     }
1118 | 
1119 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1120 | 
1121 |     try {
1122 |       // Parse externs sources.
1123 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1124 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1125 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1126 |           return null;
1127 |         }
1128 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1132 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1133 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1134 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1135 | 
1136 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1137 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1138 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1139 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1140 |           }
1141 |         }
1142 | 
1143 |         try {
1144 |           inputs =
1145 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1146 |               .manageDependencies(
1147 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1148 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1149 |           report(JSError.make(
1150 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1151 |           return null;
1152 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1153 |           report(JSError.make(
1154 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1155 |           return null;
1156 |         }
1157 |       }
1158 | 
1159 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1160 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1161 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1162 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1163 | 
1164 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1165 |         if (n == null) {
1166 |           continue;
1167 |         }
1168 | 
1169 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1170 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1171 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1172 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1173 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1174 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1175 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1176 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1177 | 
1178 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1179 | 
1180 |             externs.add(input);
1181 |             staleInputs = true;
1182 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1183 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1184 |             staleInputs = true;
1185 |           }
1186 |         }
1187 |       }
1188 | 
1189 |       if (staleInputs) {
1190 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1191 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1192 |       }
1193 | 
1194 |       // Build the AST.
1195 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1196 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1197 |         if (n == null) {
1198 |           continue;
1199 |         }
1200 | 
1201 |         if (devMode) {
1202 |           runSanityCheck();
1203 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1204 |             return null;
1205 |           }
1206 |         }
1207 | 
1208 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1209 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1210 | 
1211 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1212 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1213 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1214 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1215 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1216 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1217 |         }
1218 | 
1219 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1220 |       }
1221 | 
1222 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1223 |         return null;
1224 |       }
1225 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1226 |     } finally {
1227 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1228 |     }
1229 |   }

===== M0162 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.tryOptimizeConditionalAfterAssign(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 480-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Attempt to replace the condition of if or hook immediately that is a
   * reference to a name that is assigned immediately before.
   */
Method code:
480 |   private void tryOptimizeConditionalAfterAssign(Node n) {
481 |     Node next = n.getNext();
482 | 
483 |     // Look for patterns like the following and replace the if-condition with
484 |     // a constant value so it can later be folded:
485 |     //   var a = /a/;
486 |     //   if (a) {foo(a)}
487 |     // or
488 |     //   a = 0;
489 |     //   a ? foo(a) : c;
490 |     // or
491 |     //   a = 0;
492 |     //   a || foo(a);
493 |     // or
494 |     //   a = 0;
495 |     //   a && foo(a)
496 |     //
497 |     // TODO(johnlenz): This would be better handled by control-flow sensitive
498 |     // constant propagation. As the other case that I want to handle is:
499 |     //   i=0; for(;i<0;i++){}
500 |     // as right now nothing facilitates removing a loop like that.
501 |     // This is here simply to remove the cruft left behind goog.userAgent and
502 |     // similar cases.
503 | 
504 |     if (isSimpleAssignment(n) && isConditionalStatement(next)) {
505 |       Node lhsAssign = getSimpleAssignmentName(n);
506 | 
507 |       Node condition = getConditionalStatementCondition(next);
508 |       if (NodeUtil.isName(lhsAssign) && NodeUtil.isName(condition)
509 |           && lhsAssign.getString().equals(condition.getString())) {
510 |         Node rhsAssign = getSimpleAssignmentValue(n);
511 |         TernaryValue value = NodeUtil.getImpureBooleanValue(rhsAssign);
512 |         if (value != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
513 |           int replacementConditionNodeType =
514 |             (value.toBoolean(true)) ? Token.TRUE : Token.FALSE;
515 |           condition.getParent().replaceChild(condition,
516 |               new Node(replacementConditionNodeType));
517 |           reportCodeChange();
518 |         }
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0176 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 765-779
Documentation:

Method code:
765 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
766 |     boolean valid = false;
767 |     if (i == 0) {
768 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
769 |     } else if (i == 1) {
770 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
771 |     } else {
772 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
773 |     }
774 |     if (!valid) {
775 |       violation(
776 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
777 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
778 |     }
779 |   }

===== M0192 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processInfixExpression(InfixExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 652-667
Documentation:

Method code:
652 |     @Override
653 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
654 |       Node n =  newNode(
655 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
656 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
657 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
658 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
659 |       // doesn't do.
660 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
661 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned
662 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
663 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
664 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
665 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
666 |       return n;
667 |     }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is synthesized (i.e. not inferred).
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param inExterns {@code true} if this property was defined in an externs
   *        file. TightenTypes assumes that any function passed to an externs
   *        property could be called, so setting this incorrectly could result
   *        in live code being removed.
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the declaration of property
   *        which might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
269 |   public final boolean defineDeclaredProperty(String propertyName,
270 |       JSType type, boolean inExterns, Node propertyNode) {
271 |     boolean result = defineProperty(propertyName, type, false, inExterns,
272 |         propertyNode);
273 | 
274 |     // All property definitions go through this method
275 |     // or defineDeclaredProperty. Because the properties defined an an
276 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
277 |     // to register this after defining the property.
278 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
279 | 
280 |     return result;
281 |   }

Method code:
228 |   @Override
229 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type, boolean inferred,
230 |       boolean inExterns, Node propertyNode) {
231 |     if (hasOwnDeclaredProperty(name)) {
232 |       return false;
233 |     }
234 |     Property newProp = new Property(
235 |         name, type, inferred, inExterns, propertyNode);

239 |       // replaceScript scenario.

243 |     return true;
244 |   }

===== M0207 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 380-393
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
381 |       Node ret;
382 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
383 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
384 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
385 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
386 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
387 |       } else {
388 |         ret = transform(n);
389 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
390 |       }
391 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
392 |       return ret;
393 |     }

===== M0208 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toStringTreeHelper(Node,int,Appendable)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1139-1153
Documentation:

Method code:
1139 |   private static void toStringTreeHelper(Node n, int level, Appendable sb)
1140 |       throws IOException {
1141 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
1142 |       for (int i = 0; i != level; ++i) {
1143 |         sb.append("    ");
1144 |       }
1145 |       sb.append(n.toString());
1146 |       sb.append('\n');
1147 |       for (Node cursor = n.getFirstChild();
1148 |            cursor != null;
1149 |            cursor = cursor.getNext()) {
1150 |         toStringTreeHelper(cursor, level + 1, sb);
1151 |       }
1152 |     }
1153 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RhinoErrorReporter.replacePlaceHolders(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RhinoErrorReporter.java
Original range: 68-71
Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
68 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
69 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
70 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
71 |   }

===== M0226 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }
