===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 304-336
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
304 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
305 |     if (prototype == null) {
306 |       return false;
307 |     }
308 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
309 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
310 |       return false;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
314 |     this.prototype = prototype;
315 | 
316 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
317 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
318 |       if (superClass != null) {
319 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       if (isInterface()) {
323 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
324 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
325 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
326 |           }
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
332 |       clearCachedValues();
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     return true;
336 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 832-874
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
832 |   @Override
833 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
834 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
835 |       return true;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
839 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
840 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
841 |         return true;
842 |       }
843 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
844 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
845 |         return false;
846 |       }
847 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
848 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
849 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
850 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
851 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
852 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
853 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
854 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
855 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
856 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
857 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
858 | 
859 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
860 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
861 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
862 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
863 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
864 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
865 | 
866 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
867 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
868 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
869 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
870 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
874 |   }

===== M0264 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1488-1507
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1488 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1489 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1490 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1491 |     }
1492 | 
1493 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1494 |     Node n, n2;
1495 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1496 |          res == null && n != null;
1497 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1498 |       if (node2 == null) {
1499 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1500 |       }
1501 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1502 |       if (res != null) {
1503 |         return res;
1504 |       }
1505 |     }
1506 |     return res;
1507 |   }

===== M0284 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.optimizeSubtree(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 39-74
Documentation:
// could be changed to use code from CheckUnreachableCode to do this.
Method code:
39 |   @Override
40 |   Node optimizeSubtree(Node subtree) {
41 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
42 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
43 |         return tryFoldAssignment(subtree);
44 |       case Token.COMMA:
45 |         return tryFoldComma(subtree);
46 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
47 |       case Token.BLOCK:
48 |         return tryOptimizeBlock(subtree);
49 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
50 |         subtree = tryFoldExpr(subtree);
51 |         return subtree;
52 |       case Token.HOOK:
53 |         return tryFoldHook(subtree);
54 |       case Token.SWITCH:
55 |         return tryOptimizeSwitch(subtree);
56 |       case Token.IF:
57 |         return tryFoldIf(subtree);
58 |       case Token.WHILE:
59 |         return tryFoldWhile(subtree);
60 |        case Token.FOR: {
61 |           Node condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(subtree);
62 |           if (condition != null) {
63 |             tryFoldForCondition(condition);
64 |           }
65 |         }
66 |         return tryFoldFor(subtree);
67 |       case Token.DO:
68 |         return tryFoldDo(subtree);
69 |       case Token.TRY:
70 |         return tryFoldTry(subtree);
71 |       default:
72 |           return subtree;
73 |     }
74 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1094-1166
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1094 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1095 |     switch (type) {
1096 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1097 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1098 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1099 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1100 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1101 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1102 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1103 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1104 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1105 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1106 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1107 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1108 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1109 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1110 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1111 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1112 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1113 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1114 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1115 |       case Token.EQ:
1116 |       case Token.NE:
1117 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1118 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1119 |       case Token.LT:
1120 |       case Token.GT:
1121 |       case Token.LE:
1122 |       case Token.GE:
1123 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1124 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1125 |       case Token.LSH:
1126 |       case Token.RSH:
1127 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1128 |       case Token.SUB:
1129 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1130 |       case Token.MUL:
1131 |       case Token.MOD:
1132 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1133 |       case Token.INC:
1134 |       case Token.DEC:
1135 |       case Token.NEW:
1136 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1137 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1138 |       case Token.VOID:
1139 |       case Token.NOT:
1140 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1141 |       case Token.POS:
1142 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1143 | 
1144 |       case Token.CALL:
1145 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1146 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1147 |       // Data values
1148 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1149 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1150 |       case Token.FALSE:
1151 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1152 |       case Token.NAME:
1153 |       case Token.NULL:
1154 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1155 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1156 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1157 |       case Token.STRING:
1158 |       case Token.THIS:
1159 |       case Token.TRUE:
1160 |         return 15;
1161 | 
1162 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1163 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1164 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1165 |     }
1166 |   }

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.tryFoldExpr(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 122-137
Documentation:
/**
   * Try folding EXPR_RESULT nodes by removing useless Ops and expressions.
   * @return the replacement node, if changed, or the original if not
   */
Method code:
122 |   private Node tryFoldExpr(Node subtree) {
123 |     Node result = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(subtree.getFirstChild());
124 |     if (result == null) {
125 |       Node parent = subtree.getParent();
126 |       // If the EXPR_RESULT no longer has any children, remove it as well.
127 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
128 |         Node replacement = new Node(Token.BLOCK).copyInformationFrom(subtree);
129 |         parent.replaceChild(subtree, replacement);
130 |         subtree = replacement;
131 |       } else {
132 |         subtree.detachFromParent();
133 |         subtree = null;
134 |       }
135 |     }
136 |     return subtree;
137 |   }

===== M0312 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 723-866
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
723 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
724 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
725 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
726 |     // that we know to be safe
727 |     switch (n.getType()) {
728 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
729 |       case Token.AND:
730 |       case Token.BLOCK:
731 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
732 |       case Token.HOOK:
733 |       case Token.IF:
734 |       case Token.IN:
735 |       case Token.LP:
736 |       case Token.NUMBER:
737 |       case Token.OR:
738 |       case Token.THIS:
739 |       case Token.TRUE:
740 |       case Token.FALSE:
741 |       case Token.NULL:
742 |       case Token.STRING:
743 |       case Token.SWITCH:
744 |       case Token.TRY:
745 |       case Token.EMPTY:
746 |         break;
747 | 
748 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
749 |       case Token.THROW:
750 |         return true;
751 | 
752 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
753 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
754 |           return true;
755 |         }
756 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
757 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
758 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
759 |             return true;
760 |           }
761 |         }
762 |         return false;
763 | 
764 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
765 |       case Token.REGEXP:
766 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
767 |           return true;
768 |         }
769 |         break;
770 | 
771 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
772 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
773 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
774 |           return true;
775 |         }
776 |         break;
777 | 
778 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
779 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
780 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
781 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
782 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
783 | 
784 |       case Token.NEW:
785 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
786 |           return true;
787 |         }
788 | 
789 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
790 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
791 |           // side-effects
792 |           break;
793 |         }
794 |         return true;
795 | 
796 |       case Token.CALL:
797 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
798 |         // side effect property set.
799 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
800 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
801 |           // side-effects
802 |           break;
803 |         }
804 |         return true;
805 | 
806 |       default:
807 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
808 |           break;
809 |         }
810 | 
811 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
812 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
813 |           if (isName(assignTarget)) {
814 |             return true;
815 |           }
816 | 
817 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
818 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
819 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
820 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
821 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
822 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
823 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
824 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
825 |             return true;
826 |           }
827 | 
828 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
829 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
830 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
831 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
832 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
833 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
834 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
835 |               return false;
836 |             }
837 | 
838 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
839 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
840 |             // other objects.
841 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
842 |             // side-effect.
843 |             while (isGet(current)) {
844 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
845 |             }
846 | 
847 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
848 |           } else {
849 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
850 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
851 |             // preserve existing behavior.
852 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
853 |           }
854 |         }
855 | 
856 |         return true;
857 |     }
858 | 
859 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
860 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
861 |         return true;
862 |       }
863 |     }
864 | 
865 |     return false;
866 |   }

===== M0314 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
609 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
610 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
611 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
612 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
613 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
614 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {

624 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);

/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator

Method code:
2141 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2142 |     switch (operator) {
2143 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2144 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2145 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2146 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
2147 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
2148 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
2149 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2150 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2151 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2152 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2153 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
2154 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
2155 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2156 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2157 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2158 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
2159 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
2160 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
2161 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
2162 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
2163 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
2164 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
2165 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
2166 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
2167 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
2168 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
2169 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
2170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
2171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
2172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
2173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
2174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
2175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";

===== M0323 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 61-83
Documentation:

Method code:
61 |   @Override
62 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
63 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
64 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
65 | 
66 |     do {
67 |       somethingChanged = false;
68 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
69 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
70 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
71 | 
72 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
73 |           somethingChanged = true;
74 | 
75 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
76 |         }
77 | 
78 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
79 |           return;
80 |         }
81 |       }
82 |     } while(somethingChanged);
83 |   }

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

1700 |           break;
1701 |         case ECMASCRIPT5:

1703 |           break;
1704 |         case ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT:

1706 |           break;
1707 |         default:
1708 |           throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected language mode");
1709 |       }

1714 |         acceptConstKeyword());

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 846-859
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
846 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
847 |     if (item == null) {
848 |       return null;
849 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
850 |       return item.getNext();
851 |     } else {
852 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
853 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
854 |         return item.chain(result);
855 |       } else {
856 |         return item;
857 |       }
858 |     }
859 |   }

===== M0407 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-282
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
241 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
242 |       options.checkTypes = true;
243 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = false;
245 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
246 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
247 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
248 |       // parser type warnings.
249 |       options.setWarningLevel(
250 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
251 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
252 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
256 |       options.setWarningLevel(
257 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
258 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
262 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
263 |     guards.add(
264 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
265 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
266 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
267 | 
268 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
269 | 
270 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
271 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
272 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
273 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
274 |     // error off.
275 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
276 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
277 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
282 |   }

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

===== M0430 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.trySimpilifyUnusedResult(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 155-265
Documentation:
/**
   * General cascading unused operation node removal.
   * @param n The root of the expression to simplify.
   * @param removeUnused If true, the node is removed from the AST if
   *     it is not useful, otherwise it replaced with an EMPTY node.
   * @return The replacement node, or null if the node was is not useful.
   */
Method code:
155 |   private Node trySimpilifyUnusedResult(Node n, boolean removeUnused) {
156 |     Node result = n;
157 | 
158 |     // Simplify the results of conditional expressions
159 |     switch (n.getType()) {
160 |       case Token.HOOK:
161 |         Node trueNode = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(n.getFirstChild().getNext());
162 |         Node falseNode = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(n.getLastChild());
163 |         // If one or more of the conditional children were removed,
164 |         // transform the HOOK to an equivalent operation:
165 |         //    x() ? foo() : 1 --> x() && foo()
166 |         //    x() ? 1 : foo() --> x() || foo()
167 |         //    x() ? 1 : 1 --> x()
168 |         //    x ? 1 : 1 --> null
169 |         if (trueNode == null && falseNode != null) {
170 |           n.setType(Token.OR);
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
172 |         } else if (trueNode != null && falseNode == null) {
173 |           n.setType(Token.AND);
174 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
175 |         } else if (trueNode == null && falseNode == null) {
176 |           result = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(n.getFirstChild());
177 |         } else {
178 |           // The structure didn't change.
179 |           result = n;
180 |         }
181 |         break;
182 |       case Token.AND:
183 |       case Token.OR:
184 |         // Try to remove the second operand from a AND or OR operations:
185 |         //    x() || f --> x()
186 |         //    x() && f --> x()
187 |         Node conditionalResultNode = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(
188 |             n.getLastChild());
189 |         if (conditionalResultNode == null) {
190 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
191 |           // The conditionally executed code was removed, so
192 |           // replace the AND/OR with its LHS or remove it if it isn't useful.
193 |           result = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(n.getFirstChild());
194 |         }
195 |         break;
196 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
197 |         // A function expression isn't useful if it isn't used, remove it and
198 |         // don't bother to look at its children.
199 |         result = null;
200 |         break;
201 |       case Token.COMMA:
202 |         // We rewrite other operations as COMMA expressions (which will later
203 |         // get split into individual EXPR_RESULT statement, if possible), so
204 |         // we special case COMMA (we don't want to rewrite COMMAs as new COMMAs
205 |         // nodes.
206 |         Node left = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(n.getFirstChild());
207 |         Node right = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(n.getLastChild());
208 |         if (left == null && right == null) {
209 |           result = null;
210 |         } else if (left == null) {
211 |           result = right;
212 |         } else if (right == null){
213 |           result = left;
214 |         } else {
215 |           // The structure didn't change.
216 |           result = n;
217 |         }
218 |         break;
219 |       default:
220 |         if (!NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(n)) {
221 |           // This is the meat of this function. The node itself doesn't generate
222 |           // any side-effects but preserve any side-effects in the children.
223 |           Node resultList = null;
224 |           for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
225 |             next = c.getNext();
226 |             c = trySimpilifyUnusedResult(c);
227 |             if (c != null) {
228 |               c.detachFromParent();
229 |               if (resultList == null)  {
230 |                 // The first side-effect can be used stand-alone.
231 |                 resultList = c;
232 |               } else {
233 |                 // Leave the side-effects in-place, simplifying it to a COMMA
234 |                 // expression.
235 |                 resultList = new Node(Token.COMMA, resultList, c)
236 |                     .copyInformationFrom(c);
237 |               }
238 |             }
239 |           }
240 |           result = resultList;
241 |         }
242 |     }
243 | 
244 |     // Fix up the AST, replace or remove the an unused node (if requested).
245 |     if (n != result) {
246 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
247 |       if (result == null) {
248 |         if (removeUnused) {
249 |           parent.removeChild(n);
250 |         } else {
251 |           result = new Node(Token.EMPTY).copyInformationFrom(n);
252 |           parent.replaceChild(n, result);
253 |         }
254 |       } else {
255 |         // A new COMMA expression may not have an existing parent.
256 |         if (result.getParent() != null) {
257 |           result.detachFromParent();
258 |         }
259 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, result);
260 |       }
261 |       reportCodeChange();
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     return result;
265 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.

   *     length argument.

1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1675-1738
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1675 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1676 |     if (type != node.getType()
1677 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1678 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1679 |       return false;
1680 |     }
1681 | 
1682 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1683 |       return false;
1684 |     }
1685 | 
1686 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1687 |       try {
1688 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1689 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1690 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1691 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1692 |             return false;
1693 |           }
1694 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1695 |           return false;
1696 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1697 |           return false;
1698 |         } else {
1699 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1700 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1701 |               return false;
1702 |             }
1703 |           }
1704 |         }
1705 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1706 |         return false;
1707 |       }
1708 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1709 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1710 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1711 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1712 |         return false;
1713 |       }
1714 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1715 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1716 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1717 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1718 |         return false;
1719 |       }
1720 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1721 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1722 |         return false;
1723 |       }
1724 |     }
1725 | 
1726 |     if (recurse) {
1727 |       Node n, n2;
1728 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1729 |            n != null;
1730 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1731 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1732 |           return false;
1733 |         }
1734 |       }
1735 |     }
1736 | 
1737 |     return true;
1738 |   }

56 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
57 |     if (checkOnly) {
58 |       normalizeNodeTypes(root);
59 |     } else {
60 |       // Don't perform "PrepareAnnotations" when doing checks as

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
273 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     trace.startEvent(this);
277 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1971-1985
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1971 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1972 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1973 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1974 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
